# Kubernetes bench, twok, 2026-10-21

Raw records of the twok runs summarised in [docs/testing.md](../../../docs/testing.md#twok-results).
Each record is the file the harness writes to `TWOK_RESULTS`; only the repeated Cilium deny verdicts are cut short.
No secrets appear in them: the bench uses plain HTTP, and the Kubernetes arbiter uses the Pod's own service account token, which is never printed.

## Environment

- Cluster and members as in [baseline-2026-10-21.md](baseline-2026-10-21.md), with the twok sidecar from `ghcr.io/aenix-io/twok:sha-0588d24@sha256:3f6cf099776286cd97b60eb0340e6e24f0d9443fb95c058bc22fb70c25ba557b`, built from main at 0588d24, mode `enforce`, default timings (`claim-after` 20 s, `claim-delay` 3 s, `follower-extra-delay` 5 s, `poll-interval` 1 s).
- Partition 60 s, then at least 10 s and at most 5 minutes of watching for the pair to be restored.

## Commands

```bash
export K8S_KUBECONFIG=<kubeconfig of the test cluster>
export TWOK_IMAGE=ghcr.io/aenix-io/twok:sha-0588d24@sha256:3f6cf099776286cd97b60eb0340e6e24f0d9443fb95c058bc22fb70c25ba557b
TWOK_OVERLAY=arbiter-http hack/kubernetes-bench.sh TestPartition
TWOK_OVERLAY=arbiter-etcd hack/kubernetes-bench.sh TestPartition
TWOK_OVERLAY=arbiter-kubernetes hack/kubernetes-bench.sh TestPartition
TWOK_OVERLAY=arbiter-http hack/kubernetes-bench.sh TestPartitionOneWay
TWOK_OVERLAY=arbiter-http hack/kubernetes-bench.sh TestPartitionArbiterCut
TWOK_OVERLAY=arbiter-http hack/kubernetes-bench.sh TestSurvivorRestart
```

## 1. Two-way partition, HTTP arbiter, run 1

The first run: the metrics could not be read (the probe used a busybox binary that needs a newer glibc than the probe image has), and the twok events were not yet in the timeline; their times are in the twok logs below.

```text

namespace: twok-test, partition for 1m0s, watch for at least 10s and at most 5m0s after the heal, policies manifests/partition.yaml
```

## 6. Partition and arbiter cut from etcd-1, HTTP arbiter, with etcd-0 as leader

etcd-0 was the leader and won, as it would without the arbiter cut, so this run does not show that the cut decides; run 7 moves the leadership to etcd-1 first.
